    def if_exception_type(
        *exception_types: type[Exception],
    ) -> Callable[[Exception], bool]:
        """Creates a predicate to check if the exception is of a given type.
    
        Args:
            exception_types (Sequence[:func:`type`]): The exception types to check
                for.
    
        Returns:
            Callable[Exception]: A predicate that returns True if the provided
                exception is of the given type(s).
        """
    
        def if_exception_type_predicate(exception: Exception) -> bool:
            """Bound predicate for checking an exception type."""
            return isinstance(exception, exception_types)
    
        return if_exception_type_predicate

SCCVision/analyst/pipeline.py:611:

analysis_input = {'case_context': {'user_role': 'claimant', 'user_account': 'buyer'}, 'contact': {'name': 'Neptune Shelters', 'presence...: 'them', 'sender_name': 'Neptune Shelters', 'text': 'I charge 27750 for my services', ...}], 'system_events': [], ...}

    def call_inspect(
        analysis_input: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> tuple[ContractFacts, int]:
        """Run call 1 (full inspection). Returns (ContractFacts, calls_used).
    
        Tries parse repair first. If call 1 fails to parse, calls the inspect
        prompt a second time as a fresh attempt. calls_used includes only
        successful-or-attempted API calls (not 429 retries).
        """
        user_content = _build_inspect_user(analysis_input)
        calls_used = 0
        last_error: Exception | None = None
    
        for attempt in range(MAX_CALLS):
            try:
                raw = _groq_call(INSPECT_PROMPT, user_content, seed=1001)
                calls_used += 1
            except Exception as exc:
                calls_used += 1
                last_error = exc
                break
    
            try:
                data = _parse_json(raw)
                facts = ContractFacts.model_validate(data)
                return facts, calls_used
            except (AnalystError, json.JSONDecodeError, ValidationError) as exc:
                last_error = exc
                if attempt == 0:
                    # Let the second attempt try fresh (not in the retry loop of the
                    # caller) — but only if we haven't burned both call slots.
                    continue
                break
    
>       raise AnalystError(
            f"Inspect call failed to parse after {calls_used} attempt(s): {last_error}"
        ) from last_error
E       SCCVision.analyst.client.AnalystError: Inspect call failed to parse after 1 attempt(s): No GROQ_API_KEY configured
